# ASTROLABE — implementation TODO (Kotlin/JVM SDK)

**Status: implementation in progress. P0 is complete and `FIXTURE_VALIDATED` (both CI platforms green, 2026-09-21); P1's cell runtime is under construction, so the S0 agent is not yet integrated.** Spec authority: the architecture documents under `docs/` reached from [SOTA-BEST-MIXED-AGENT.md](SOTA-BEST-MIXED-AGENT.md). This file sequences the build and records progress; where it seems to conflict with a subsystem document, the document wins and the conflict becomes a `D-nn` item in [§3](#3-decisions-and-open-items). Owner decisions and their reasons are recorded in [ANSWERS.md](ANSWERS.md); plan defects found in review, with their regression fixtures, are in [ISSUES.md](ISSUES.md) (`I-nn`, tracked here as `IX-nn` rows in [§5](#5-fixture-map)). Both are decision records, not specification.

## 0 How to use this file

### 0.1 Resume protocol (every session)
Workflow, task selection, verification tiers, commands and session state are defined only in [CLAUDE.md](CLAUDE.md) § Workflow (auto-loaded with the [handoff](CONTINUE-TASK.md)). This file stays the progress authority: task statuses, `Log:` lines, `Deps`, §2.4 producers, `D-nn` decisions.

**Precedence (2026-09-24):** per-task full-build, CI or both-platform steps written in task entries (`Done:`/`Log:` wording such as "ABI/full build", "green on both platforms") are discharged at the block gate (`- [ ] **Gate …**` lines at the end of each unfinished work package, see CLAUDE.md); a task is `DONE` when its `Build`/`Done` criteria pass with focused tests. A failing gate reopens only the tasks it implicates. Phase-validation tasks (P1.12.4, P2.7.x, …) are themselves gate work.

### 0.2 IDs, status, tags
- **IDs** are stable: phase `P<n>`, work package `P<n>.<m>`, task `P<n>.<m>.<k>`, decision `D-nn`, runtime fixture `FX-nn` ([§5](#5-fixture-map)), adapter fixture `AX-nn`, issue-regression fixture `IX-nn` (from `ISSUES.md` I-nn).
- **Status** is the last token of a task heading after `·`: `TODO` · `IN_PROGRESS` · `BLOCKED(D-nn|reason)` · `DONE`. Work-package and phase status is derived (all tasks `DONE`).
- **Completion levels** (three, never conflated — I-19, [§19.1](docs/evaluation/method.md#sec-19-1)): `IMPLEMENTED` (named artifacts and integration paths exist) → `FIXTURE_VALIDATED` (the phase's FX/AX/IX harness tests pass on Windows and Linux) → `PROMOTED` (its predeclared live quality/cost/regression gate passed). Under this plan every live gate stays **`UNMEASURED`** (P7); optional layers stay off for normal use until promoted; mandatory correctness controls are never disabled in a production configuration (D-48).
- **Kind tag** after the ID: `[C]` contract/scaffold (types, interfaces, storage schema) · `[M]` minimal working implementation · `[I]` integration between components · `[V]` validation/hardening (fixtures, fault injection) · `[O]` optional/deferred (carries its evaluation gate; ships behind a `Config` flag, off by default).
- **Task fields** (only where useful): `Why` · `Deps` · `Pkg` (module/package) · `Build` (expected types/functions) · `Spec` (doc sections, fixtures, `I-nn`) · `Notes` · `Done` (completion criteria) · `Log` (appended by the implementer: date, decisions, files).
- Numbers in tasks are the [declared defaults §17](docs/reference/defaults.md#sec-17); all live in `Config`, none hard-coded.

### 0.3 Scope boundary of this plan
In scope: the whole architecture through S3 as a library, validated with a **fake provider adapter**. Out of scope ([P7](#p7-deferred-out-of-scope-boundary)): live provider transports, auth, retry/backoff, HTTP clients, MCP client transports, confined-runner backends, live benchmark campaigns. Every phase states which fixtures it must pass and which platform/provider assumptions remain unsupported. The roadmap rule "no stage starts before the previous gate is measured" ([§18.2](docs/implementation/roadmap.md#sec-18-2)) is interpreted for this offline plan as: engineering proceeds through P6 with each live gate recorded `UNMEASURED` and its prerequisites named; promotion claims wait for live evaluation (I-19, D-28).

### 1.1 Targeted readiness audit (2026-09-20)

Scope: resolve contradictory progress counts, completion claims and integration notes in `TODO.md`, `actual_state.md` and `CONTINUE-TASK.md`. Baseline: `468f5b0` (implementation checkpoint `0b94875`). This was not a whole-codebase review or a fresh full build; production code and tests were not changed.

| Disputed claim | Executed/inspected evidence | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| No implementation / P0 has 26 completed tasks | Existing compiled core/provider JARs; SkeletonTest executes the Java fixture; FakeAdapterTest executes scripted invocations; only 19 distinct P0 task headings exist | Remove the obsolete banner and incorrect count |
| P1 has 45 completed tasks and 17 remaining | 44 DONE headings, 19 TODO; DeriveS0Test, CoherenceTest, RoleTest, DispatcherTest and KbToolTest passed | Correct arithmetic; retain P1 task statuses |
| Agent runtime or Java SDK may already be ready | `javap` exposes only INSTANCE/MODULE/VERSION on Astrolabe; `jar tf` shows role types only in cell and no campaign/telemetry/AstrolabeJava; JavaConsumerSmoke only reads MODULE | P1.8.2–P1.8.8, P1.9.*, P1.11.*, P1.12.* remain TODO |
| Seven tool families imply a working KB | KbToolTest exercises EmptyKb and a test-supplied Kb; persistent note store is absent | P1.6.10 is a completed contract, not completion of P2.6/P4.1 |
| Tool-level wiring still entirely pending | LookTest (9), EditTest (10), RunTest (6) pass; inspected redaction, masks, guards, env and version-change calls | Separate existing component wiring from remaining runtime integration in §1 |
| CI not run / P0 completely validated | GitHub API lists two failed runs; latest run at this exact baseline fails on both platforms | Reopen P0.1.2, P0.6.1, P0.6.2, P0.6.4; retain their implemented code |

**Local executions:** Windows / JDK 26 / cached Gradle 9.7.1, offline. First batch: 31 tests (Skeleton 1, FakeAdapter 11, DeriveS0 5, Coherence 4, Role 3, Dispatcher 5, KbTool 2). Second batch: 28 tests (Look 9, Edit 10, Run 6, three isolated CI failures 1 each). All **59 passed, zero failures/errors/skips**. Both `:core:test` tasks executed rather than returning UP-TO-DATE. The second run replaces the first run's local core XML reports; historical 773-test reports are not the current filtered report. The 773/0-failures/6-skips result belongs to the historical session-2 local run, while 675 belongs to session 1.

Reproduce the two batches in PowerShell (the installed JDK/cache paths are machine-specific):

```powershell
$env:JAVA_HOME = 'C:\Users\user\.gradle\jdks\eclipse_adoptium-26-amd64-windows.2'
$env:GRADLE_USER_HOME = 'C:\Users\user\.gradle'
./gradlew.bat :core:test --tests io.astrolabe.SkeletonTest --tests io.astrolabe.fixtures.FakeAdapterTest --tests io.astrolabe.contract.DeriveS0Test --tests io.astrolabe.evidence.CoherenceTest --tests io.astrolabe.cell.RoleTest --tests io.astrolabe.tool.DispatcherTest --tests io.astrolabe.tool.kb.KbToolTest --offline --console=plain --no-configuration-cache
./gradlew.bat :core:test --tests io.astrolabe.tool.look.LookTest --tests io.astrolabe.tool.edit.EditTest --tests io.astrolabe.tool.run.RunTest --tests 'io.astrolabe.fixtures.FixtureReposTest.ts-small passes*' --tests 'io.astrolabe.os.GitTest.update index refuses*' --tests 'io.astrolabe.os.ProcOwnershipTest.the execution deadline kills*' --offline --console=plain --no-configuration-cache
```

**Remote evidence:** [CI run 35514932596](https://github.com/korvin2000/ASTROLABE/actions/runs/35514932596), commit `468f5b0903308733f32d15e9ab3b73674d779e72`. Ubuntu fails with `./gradlew: Permission denied` (exit 126) before tests; `git ls-files -s gradlew` confirms mode `100644`. Windows reaches core tests: **758 tests, 3 failed, 130 skipped**. Failures:

- `FixtureReposTest`: TS JUnit output lacks the expected `index.test.ts` filename.
- `GitTest`: `updateIndex` does not throw the expected exception when passed the repository index.
- `ProcOwnershipTest`: the deadline scenario never observes `GRANDCHILD=<pid>` in its log.

All three pass in the isolated local rerun; their remote failures remain unresolved. No claim is made yet about the Windows root causes. Local passes do not validate Linux or restore the CI gate. The earlier [CI run 35490557390](https://github.com/korvin2000/ASTROLABE/actions/runs/35490557390) also has failure status. Next work is P0 validation repair/reproduction, then P1.8.2; live gates remain `UNMEASURED`.

**Diagnosis and repair (2026-09-21).** Root causes come from run 35514932596's own job logs and its `test-reports-windows-latest` artifact, not from reruns; each repair carries a test that fails without it.

| Recorded failure | Root cause (evidence) | Repair | Owner |
|---|---|---|---|
| Ubuntu `./gradlew: Permission denied` (126) | `gradlew` recorded `100644`; the file itself is fine (LF blob, `.gitattributes eol=lf`), only the mode is wrong, which is invisible on Windows | mode set to `100755`; `SkeletonTest` asserts `git ls-files -s gradlew` reports it | P0.1.2 |
| `GitTest`: `updateIndex` did not refuse `.git/index` | **Product defect.** The D-53 guard compared path spellings; the runner's `TEMP` is a short (8.3) name while `rev-parse --absolute-git-dir` prints the long one, so the user's index passed the guard | `requireTemporaryIndex` compares canonical paths and also protects the common dir's index; new `GitTest` case builds a second spelling (symlink, else 8.3) and fails against the old guard | P0.6.2 |
| `FixtureReposTest`: no `index.test.ts` in the junit output | Runner Node is v22.23.2, whose junit reporter emits a flat `<testcase classname="test"/>` list with **no** `file` attribute; the reported suite itself passed 7/7. The D-27/D-50/IX-13 guarantee needs Node 24 | assertion split into its own test gated by `Runners.nodeMajor()`; workflow pins Node 24 | P0.6.4 |
| `ProcOwnershipTest`: `GRANDCHILD=` never appeared | Test race, not lost ownership. The artifact times the failing case at 10.077s against 28.9/20.9/19.7s for the three grandchild tests that allow 45s, so the launcher needed roughly 20s to first output and the fixed 10s deadline killed the tree first — which is the specified behaviour | deadline sized from a measured `ChildCommands.launcherReady()` probe; `awaitLogMatch` now reports status and elapsed time | P0.6.1 |
| 130 Windows skips | 128 are `rg is not on PATH`: the ripgrep backend and its parity suite never ran on CI, so P0.6.3's both-backends criterion was unexercised there | workflow installs ripgrep on both runners and prints `rg --version` | P0.1.2 |

Also repaired: the FX-22 intermittency recorded under P0.6.1 (local, not CI). Two wall-clock races in `RunTest` — the observation-timeout assertion relied on the child's 6s sleep outlasting the intervening polls and store work, and the completion assertion required the poll delivering `bg-end` to also observe the shell exit. Both reproduce locally once the first race is removed; the timeout assertion moved onto the silent minute-long handle and completion polls until the status settles.

Local verification (Windows / JDK 26 / Gradle 9.7.1, offline): full `build` — core **878 tests, 0 failures/errors, 6 existing platform skips**, eval **30**, provider-api **15**; ABI dumps unchanged (the changes are private or in `testFixtures`).

**Resolved (2026-09-21).** Two further rounds were needed, both on this test's own assumptions rather than on the harness. Run 35621166065 reached both suites for the first time — Windows 878/1/2 (from 758/3/130), Linux 878/1/3 (from: never started) — failing only on an FX-22 assertion that the background child was still `running`, which holds just while its sleep outlasts the poll. Run 35622134003 then failed one Windows case: the measured deadline of the `ProcOwnership` repair could exceed the root's fixed 120s lifetime, so it exited 0 before the deadline fired — the mirror image of the original race. The child's lifetime is now derived from the deadline (capped at 90s) instead of being a constant. [CI run 35623690223](https://github.com/korvin2000/ASTROLABE/actions/runs/35623690223) is green on both platforms at `8052946`: Ubuntu 2m02 / three platform skips, Windows 8m45 / two. **P0.1.2, P0.6.1, P0.6.2 and P0.6.4 are DONE and P0 is `FIXTURE_VALIDATED`**; every live gate stays `UNMEASURED`.

## 2 Target structure and conventions

### 2.1 Gradle modules (create a module only when its first task starts)
| Module | Artifact | Purpose | Phase |
|---|---|---|---|
| `build-logic/` | — | convention plugin `astrolabe.kotlin-library`: Kotlin 2.4.20 with `-Xjdk-release=26`, Java `--release 26`, JDK 26 toolchain for compile, tests and the Gradle daemon (D-02), Gradle **9.7.0** wrapper pinned with `distributionSha256Sum`, explicit dependency versions in the catalog, `explicitApi()`, `abiValidation()`, `java-library`, `java-test-fixtures`, `maven-publish`, JUnit 5 | P0 |
| `provider-api/` | `astrolabe-provider-api` | item model, capabilities, adapter SPI (Kotlin + Java forms), invocation ids, billable usage/price types, tool schema types, token-estimation abstraction. **No networking; no dependency on `core`.** Future provider modules depend only on this. | P0 |
| `core/` | `astrolabe-core` | everything else (one process, packages below); `testFixtures` = fake adapter, temp repos, scripted model, fault injection | P0–P5 |
| `index-treesitter/` | `astrolabe-index-treesitter` | tier-1 symbol index/syntax via `io.github.tree-sitter:ktreesitter` (native binary; optional) | P5 |
| `eval/` | `astrolabe-eval` | evaluation runner: fixture suites, frozen campaigns, research arms, scorecard, promotion; separate entry point | P6 |
| deferred | `astrolabe-provider-openai`, `-anthropic`, `-compat`, `astrolabe-mcp-client`, `astrolabe-runner-confined-*`, `astrolabe-cli` | see P7 | — |

Libraries (pin explicit versions at P0.1.1): `kotlinx-coroutines-core` (+`-jdk8` for `CompletableFuture` bridges), `kotlinx-serialization-json`, `org.xerial:sqlite-jdbc` (bundles FTS5), `org.slf4j:slf4j-api`, JUnit 5 + `kotlin-test`; optional `com.knuddels:jtokkit` (OpenAI-family token estimates, D-06), `io.github.tree-sitter:ktreesitter` (P5). OS bindings use the JDK's `java.lang.foreign` (FFM) API, no JNA (D-43). No Spring, no Exposed/SQLDelight (D-03), no JGit (D-04), no YAML library (D-24).

### 2.3 Code conventions (binding for every task)
- **Kotlin style:** `explicitApi()`; `data class`/`sealed interface` for records and outcomes; `enum` for closed vocabularies from the docs (status words, effect classes, kinds); no `value class` in public API (Java mangling, D-08); no Kotlin `Result` in public API; typed ids validated non-empty/canonical at construction; immutable collections or defensive copies at authority boundaries; `fun interface` for callbacks; `@JvmOverloads`/`@JvmStatic` where Java callers need them.
- **Async and Java (D-07, I-14):** one `suspend`/`Flow` engine inside `core`; every host-implemented SPI (`ProviderAdapter`, `Authority`, `EventSink`) has a Java-implementable form (`CompletableFuture`-based or synchronous) with documented cancellation propagation, exception mapping, callback threading and resource closure; the `io.astrolabe.java` package contains no `suspend`, `Flow` or `value class`.
- **Names:** docs vocabulary verbatim (`Increment`, `Receipt`, `Workset`, `Stamp`), precise verbs, no `Manager/Impl/Helper/Service` suffixes (the docs' "delegation service" is `Delegator`), explicit units in names (`tokens`, `seconds`, `bytes`).
- **Structure as documentation:** comments only for non-obvious invariants, protocol constraints (e.g. "assistant calls precede their results"), and the doc section they enforce (`// §8.7`).
- **Algorithms over frameworks:** sorted interval lists for displayed ranges; hash sets/maps for closures and joins; BFS over the import graph for blast radius; Kahn/Tarjan for requirement-graph and note-dependency cycles; bounded heaps for eviction order and note top-k; explicit state machines (`Lifecycle`, `Intent`, `Proc`, `Invocation`) with typed transitions; parse tool args and runner output once at the boundary into internal types.
- **Determinism:** every policy (`select_shape`, `select_profile`, `compile`, eviction, gates) is a pure function of records, logged with its inputs; `Clock` and `IdGen` injected; no wall-clock or counters in cached prompt regions; capture timestamps are metadata, never part of an identity (I-05).
- **Ownership (L9):** one writer per store table; controller commits ledger, verifier accepts, curator publishes notes, runner assigns tool status — never the model. One controller process per project store (D-44).
- **Evidence and paths:** `FileVersion` hashes raw bytes, metadata caches are hints (I-05); every built-in filesystem operation goes through the `WorkspacePath` contract (D-47); redacted bytes never grant coverage (D-49).
- **Tests:** JUnit 5 + kotlin-test; fixtures from [§19.3](docs/evaluation/fixtures.md#sec-19-3) and `ISSUES.md` are harness tests first; no network; fake adapter only; Windows and Linux are equal targets and both run in CI from P0 (D-12).
- **Storage rule:** SQLite is canonical for structured records; Markdown/JSON under `exports/` and `kb/*.md` are derived views ([§4](docs/state/contracts.md#sec-4)); durability policy per D-44.
