#### P1.7.4 [M] Receipt emission and currency · DONE
- Why: [§8.4](docs/verification/scheduler.md#sec-8-4) refined by D-45 (I-04): a receipt supports the candidate actually tested.
- Build: `Scheduler.record(run/verify result)` → `Receipt` with `stamp_before/after`, argv/cwd, env, verifier version, definition version, parsed counts, limits, closure, `tested_inputs` (paths@versions from the declared closure ∪ observed access) and `input_stability ∈ {exclusive, isolated, unknown}`; `exclusive` = the workspace mutation lock is held for the whole check and a post-check rescan of `tested_inputs` (raw hashes) shows no write by any subprocess or external writer; eligibility: a required acceptance receipt counts as current only with `exclusive|isolated` stability, no relevant input mutation during the check, and `stampAfter == stampNow` (necessary, not sufficient); a mutation during the check keeps the factual outcome and marks the receipt `ineligible-for-final-tree` ⇒ rerun; declared scratch/output policy prevents spurious invalidation; stale marking through `Coherence`; reuse proofs arrive in P3.1.2.
- Done: a failed command cannot become a green receipt; exit 0 wrapper never green (FX-08); passing test followed by source mutation, mutate-and-restore background writer, and unknown stability never certify untested final bytes (IX-04).
- Log: 2026-09-20 — evidence.Receipts seam (InMemory, `SqliteReceipts` over the `receipts` table; the raw blob must be published first — the FK enforces §4.3 ordering). verify.Scheduler(checks, workspace, registry, stamper, receipts, aliases, idGen, ids, clock, verifierVersion = Astrolabe.VERSION, scratch = ScratchPolicy()).runCheck(check, contractVersion, inputs = [], execute: suspend () -> Executed): holds `Workspace.mutation` for the whole check; tested inputs = the declared closure (Known paths; Package = directory walk ∪ inputs; Unknown = the caller's enumeration, e.g. atlas rows) minus `ScratchPolicy` prefixes (caches, build/dist/target, reports); each input is hashed **and** stat-ed before and rescanned after, so a write by the command, a background writer or a human lands in `tested_inputs.mutated_during_check` — a restore-after-write leaves the bytes equal but moves the metadata and is caught too (IX-04); `input_stability = exclusive` (lock + rescan) or `unknown` when an unknown closure has no enumeration (limit recorded); the factual outcome stays, a `Passed` without executed counts becomes `Inconclusive` (FX-08/D-50); moved paths announced through `announceMoved`; `Receipt` recorded with stamps, env id, verifier/definition/contract versions, counts, closure, tested inputs, raw blob, limits, exit code; `#n` alias (kind `receipt`, `aliasOf`); `Checks.record(LastResult(receiptId, stampAfter, definitionVersion, outcome, counts, Current))`. `Executed(command, cwd, shell, exit, outcome, counts, raw, limits)` is what a `RunResult`/`CheckerResult`/`Shaped` is adapted into by the caller. `currency(check, stampNow)` → `Currency(receiptId, applicability via Checks.refresh, eligible per D-45, green, reasons)` with `certifies` = current ∧ eligible ∧ green; a later tree move ⇒ stale "(no reuse proof)" until P3.1.2. Not here: `isolated` candidates (P3.1.5), reuse proofs (P3.1.2), a subprocess-level writer fence (trusted-local cannot provide one — the rescan is the detector, D-45 as refined). Tests: SchedulerTest over a temp repo + SQLite receipts (clean receipt certifies then goes stale; FX-08 wrapper/uncounted; IX-04 mutation and restore-after-write; unknown closure with/without enumeration; scratch writes never invalidate but do move the stamp; package enumeration and scratch policy).

#### P1.7.5 [M] `Baseline` receipt and pre-existing-failure ledger · DONE
- Why: [§8.5](docs/verification/scheduler.md#sec-8-5); the evidence line "pre-existing failure requires a baseline receipt".
- Deps: P1.2.3, P1.2.4 (materialize the captured initial candidate into `candidates/` from snapshot 0 and verify it against its manifest before the run, D-53), P1.6.6 (namespaced test identities)
- Build: `Baseline.run(suite)` on the verified initial candidate at `s0` → `PreexistingLedger(entries: namespaced test identity + environment fingerprint + failure signature, with multiplicity)`; rendered once in `[K]`; later red matching ⇒ `pre-existing (unchanged)`, else steering; ambiguous identity never matches (D-50); never permission to waive task acceptance; exceptions explicit in the finish receipt.
- Done: baseline runs against the captured tree even after edits; identity+signature matching, not counts; same-name tests in two modules stay distinct (IX-13).
- Log: 2026-09-20 — verify.Baseline(shadowRef, layout, runner, os, receipts, aliases, blobs, redaction, estimator, idGen, ids, clock, env, verifierVersion, envAllowlist, scratch).run(check, contractVersion, s0, timeoutSeconds = 600) → BaselineResult(receipt, ledger?, candidateDir, materialized): snapshot 0 is materialized under `candidates/<work>-<attempt>-s0` and verified against its manifest first (mismatch ⇒ `unavailable` receipt, no run, D-53); every exported file is a tested input (the manifest holds only the dirty overlay; HEAD files come from the object store) hashed before the run and re-verified after, so a suite that rewrote its own copy is `mutated_during_check` and ineligible (`input_stability = isolated`, D-45); the suite runs with cwd = the candidate, CI/NO_COLOR, the deadline kills at `timeoutSeconds`; capture redacted into a `LOG` blob, fresh JUnit XML under `build/test-results` / `target/*-reports` collected as invocation-bound `ReportArtifact`s (D-50), shaped by `Shapers`; `Receipt` bound to `stamp_before = stamp_after = s0`, the env id, verifier/definition/contract versions; `#n` alias. `PreexistingLedger(receiptId, alias, stamp, envId, entries: PreexistingFailure(identity, signature, multiplicity), ambiguous, limitations)` from the failing identities, `signatureOf` = first message line with only addresses/long hex/timestamps/temp paths normalized (D-19); `classify(result, envId)` → PreExisting (same identity ∧ same signature ∧ same env) | New | Changed(baseline signature) | Ambiguous (duplicate identity in the baseline or a different environment) — same-name tests in other modules and other parameterizations are `New` (IX-13); `render()` once for `[K]`, bounded. A timeout/unavailable/unknown baseline has **no** ledger: nothing may be called pre-existing without a usable receipt. The ledger never waives task acceptance (the exit gate, P1.7.7, reads it only to label reds). Tests: BaselineTest over a real shadow ref + SQLite receipts with a recorded pytest output printed by the candidate (edits after capture do not reach the baseline; counts, stamps, isolated stability, ledger identity/signature/env matching; volatile-data normalization; a self-mutating suite is ineligible; timeout and missing runner yield no ledger).

#### P1.7.6 [M] `Reserve` enforcement · DONE
- Why: [§8.1 reserve](docs/verification/scheduler.md#sec-8-1); FX-43.
- Deps: P0.2.2
- Build: verification 15 % + recovery/persist 5 % of the cell's tokens and turns held from cell start (raised to known check costs); the 15 % is spendable only on checks and the final register patch, the 5 % only on the Result Packet, receipts and the STATUS note; when only the reserve remains the `reserve reached` gate blocks new edits and generation ("verify and report"); reserve reached with checks outstanding ⇒ `partial` naming the unverified scope.
- Done: FX-43; reserve spend never on generation.
- Log: 2026-09-20 — budget.CellBudget(tokens, turns, reserve: CellReserve, events?, ids?) / `CellBudget.of(cellTokens, cellTurns, reserves, knownCheckCostTokens)`: three `Reservations` partitions held from cell start — `working` = tokens − reserves, `verification` (raised to known check costs), `recovery`; `Spend` enum (Generation, Edit, Check, RegisterPatch, ResultPacket, Receipt, StatusNote) with a draw order per purpose: generation/edit → working only; check/register patch → working then verification; packet/receipt/status note → working then recovery; `admit(spend, estimate)` → Admitted(partitions, reconcile(actual)/release()) splitting across the allowed partitions in draw order, or Refused with the reason (generation refused with "reserve reached: … the reserves are not spendable on it"); turns partitioned the same way — `startTurn(spend)`: generation turns = turns − reserve turns, then only verify-and-report spends proceed on the reserve turns; `reserveReached` = no working tokens or no generation turns; `verdict(outstandingChecks)` → ReserveVerdict(reached, gate = "reserve reached: verify and report; no new edits", partial = "partial: reserve reached with checks outstanding — unverified: …") (FX-43); `snapshot()` for the gauge (percent used, reserveOk, turns taken); `Budget.Reserved/Exhausted` events. Enforced across concurrent calls by `Reservations`; reconciled to actual usage with overrun tracking. Wiring owner: the cell loop (P1.8.7) admits every generation/edit/check/report spend through it and the dispatcher's read budget stays the per-turn look budget. Tests: CellBudgetTest (partitions and purposes, generation never on reserve, split admissions, known-cost raise, turn gate and `partial` naming the unverified scope).

#### P1.7.7 [M] `ExitGate` and `Verifier.accept` · DONE
- Why: [§8.7](docs/verification/acceptance-review.md#sec-8-7), [§5.9](docs/runtime/gates-termination.md#sec-5-9); F8; F01 corrections (committed authority, criterion-bound current evidence, required review approval).
- Build: `ExitGate.evaluate(register, contract, increment, receipts, reviews, flags) → Accepted | Refused(missing: list)`: every `run:` acceptance green *and current*; every `check:` has an accepted assessment of the stated criterion (not merely an id); every `review:` signed (human path P3, judge P4); no red verify line without an `Open` item; no `[ ]`/`[>]` without disposition; acceptance-surface flags (P1.7.8 baseline; P3.4.2 precision) justified **and** reviewed through a supported path when they touch a required check; no unresolved impact nudge for a changed public definition (P3.2.4); only receipts eligible per D-45 count. `Verifier.accept(packet)` validates status against receipts (never the model's word), binds `base_stamp`, `patch_hash`, `resulting_stamp`, env; repeated unsupported finalization ⇒ gap-directed recovery, not an endless gate; budget/cancellation/process-wait are explicit non-completed exits.
- Done: gate refusal lists exactly what is missing; `Open` cannot waive a required acceptance; ledger untouched on refusal.
- Log: 2026-09-20 — verify.ExitGate.evaluate(register, contract, increment, currencies: Map<checkId|acceptanceId, Currency>, assessments, reviews: Map<acceptanceId, Verdict>, flags, unresolvedImpactNudges) → GateResult.Accepted(receiptIds) | Refused(missing): per increment `accept` item — `run:` needs a `Currency.certifies` (current ∧ D-45 eligible ∧ green; the reasons name why not: no receipt, stale (no reuse proof), inputs moved, outcome failed), `check:` needs an `Assessment` that is accepted, names the stated criterion text and binds this contract version, `review:` needs an approving signed `Verdict` at this contract version; a red non-required check needs an unclosed `Open` item naming it; every `[ ]`/`[>]` step is a missing disposition; an acceptance-surface flag on a required check needs both a recorded justification and an approving verdict (P1.7.8 baseline); each unresolved impact nudge is listed (P3.2.4 supplies them). An `Open` item never waives a required item. `Verifier(maxFinalizations = 2).accept(proposal: CompletionProposal(incrementId, claimedStatus, contractVersion, baseStamp, resultingStamp, patchHash, envId, reason), contract, increment, register, ledger, stampNow, …)` → Accepted(bound stamps/patch/env, receiptIds, new Ledger with the increment's requirements `verified` + evidence + stampValid) | Refused(missing incl. contract-version and resulting-stamp-vs-now mismatches, attempts, `recoveryDirected` after `maxFinalizations`) | NotCompleted(ExitKind blocked|partial|replan|waiting|budget_exhausted|cancelled, reason): the claim is never the condition, the input ledger is immutable and untouched on refusal, an unknown status word is a protocol error. Wiring owner: the cell (P1.8.8) builds the proposal from the Result Packet and the controller (P1.9.3) commits the returned ledger and emits `IncrementClosed`. Tests: ExitGateTest (acceptance with receipts, every missing-reason text, Open never waives, assessment/review binding, undisposed steps, red-without-Open, surface flags justified+reviewed, nudges; verifier stamp/version binding, ledger untouched then updated, recovery after two refusals, distinct non-completed exits).

- Log: 2026-09-20 — P2.1.1 integration refinement: accepted completion now binds work/attempt/context, contract revision and increment definition; register/increment mismatch rejected. Preserve check assessment/review refs alongside run receipt IDs. Verifier rejects review/required integrity approvals for another candidate (and integrity approvals for another contract revision); the new stale-review regression failed before the fix and passed after it. S0 callers remain source-compatible; graph/controller currency and aggregate requirement status are distinct.

#### P1.7.8 [M] Baseline `ScopeGuard` and conservative acceptance-surface policy (S0) · DONE
- Why: invariants 1–2 and [§8.6](docs/verification/acceptance-review.md#sec-8-6) are baseline obligations, not deep analysis; S0 must not mutate before they exist (I-02, D-31); P3.4 adds precision and workflows.
- Deps: P1.1.1, P1.2.6, P0.4.2
- Pkg: `verify`
- Build: `ScopeGuard.check(edit, contract)`: every built-in edit path must lie inside the committed `contract.scope.write_paths` (real path, D-47) and outside `protected_paths` (D-class); outside ⇒ refused until an amendment is approved and committed (a pending proposal grants nothing); already-authorized CI/lockfile/migration work is represented in the committed contract, not blocked by a blanket prompt. `TestIntegrity.baseline(diff)`: conservative classifier — any edit or run touching known test files, check definitions (`pytest.ini`, `jest.config.*`, `conftest.py`, build test config, CI files) or acceptance commands ⇒ `TestIntegrityFlag(kind = unclassified-weakening-risk)` rendered as the acceptance-surface line; completion with an unresolved flag on a required check needs `Authority.review` approval (human path) with the original obligation attached, otherwise the increment stays unaccepted; unknown classification is never proof of no weakening; legitimate test edits pass through review, not silence.
- Done: S0 fixtures: an uncommitted scope expansion is rejected; a weakened required check cannot complete without a review verdict; mid-attempt configuration changes are ignored (with P1.9.4) — all before P2/P3 exist (IX-02); FX-14/FX-52 pass in their baseline form.
- Log: 2026-09-20 — workspace.PathPattern = the one scope convention (`dir/` prefix, bare `name` anywhere, slashed path = itself or below, globs with `**`, `**` = repository); `Scope.covers/protects/allowsWrite` use it. verify.ScopeGuard(workspace).check(paths, committedContract, increment?) → ScopeVerdict.Allowed(contractVersion, resolved paths, outsideIncrement) | Refused(contractVersion, refusals: Protected | OutsideContract | PathRejected): every path goes through `Workspace.resolve(Intent.Mutate)` (protected targets by real path, D-47) and then the contract's own protected list and write scope; the guard reads only the contract it is handed, so `amendmentsPending` grants nothing and a widening takes effect only as a committed new version (FX-52 baseline, IX-02); one refusal refuses the batch. verify.TestIntegrity.baseline(touched, cause, contract, checks) → TestIntegrityFlag(path, surface TestFile | CheckDefinition | CiConfig | AcceptanceCommand, cause, requiredChecks, kind = unclassified-weakening-risk, reason?, verdict?) with the acceptance-surface `line`; surfaces: CI prefixes/names, manifest + test-config names/prefixes (`CHECK_DEFINITION_*`), `atlas.isTestPath` (made public: the one "known test file" rule), paths named by an acceptance command's argv/cwd; required checks tied conservatively (definition/CI ⇒ all required; test/command input ⇒ Unknown closure, Known/Package containing the path, or argv naming it). `blocksCompletion` = tied required check without an approving verdict; `reviewRequest(...)` carries the criteria plus the original obligations (acceptance definitions with origin + obligation version, D-52, and per-path `originals` such as a preimage id); `resolve(request, flags, authority)` attaches the human-path verdict (`AutonomousAuthority.review` = null ⇒ stays unaccepted, FX-14 baseline). Not here: the "warning once, justification on repeat" count (cell gates, P1.8.5), the exit-gate consumption of `unresolved` (P1.7.7), precise weakening kinds (P3.4.2), and the mid-attempt configuration part of IX-02 (`AttemptConfig.freeze`, P1.9.4 — both guards are pure functions of the committed records they are handed). New debt for P1.9.2: bind the workspace's `ProtectedPaths` to the committed contract's protected list at campaign open (until then the path contract fails closed on authorized migration/CI/lock-file work; tested in ScopeGuardTest).

### P1.8 Cell runtime
#### P1.8.1 [C] `Role` configuration · DONE
- Why: [§3.4](docs/architecture/roles-shapes.md#sec-3-4) role = context view × note scope × skill filter × tool mask × permission × tier prior × duties × ask-back × packet; declared in configuration, not code; never a security boundary.
- Pkg: `cell`
- Build: `Role(name, contextView, noteScope, skillFilter, toolMask, permission, tierPrior, duties, askBack, packetKind, personaLines ≤ 3)`; `Roles.implementing` default; effective ops = role mask ∩ shape/stage ∩ authorization.
- Done: roles loadable from `Config`; effective-ops intersection test.
- Log: 2026-09-20 — cell.Role (serializable: name, contextView: Set<ContextPart>, noteScope, skillFilter, toolMask, permission: Stage, tierPrior: Tier, duties, askBack, packetKind: PacketKind, personaLines ≤ 3, policyTextVersion "roles/1" for fingerprints — D-38) with `effectiveOps(shape, ceiling)` = role mask ∩ `Roles.shapeMask(shape)` ∩ ops the authorization's capability set allows (`Ceiling.allows`); `Roles` declares the §3.4 table (implementing, plan, probe, review, qa, writer, repair, extractor) and the shape masks (S0 = `ToolOps.implementingS0`, S1 + propose/kb.propose, S2 − transform, S3 all; only S0 exercised in P1). `Config.roles: Map<String, Role>` + `Config.role(name)` (override else SDK default); `Config.violations` refuses an override that widens the mask, raises the permission, changes the packet, misnames its key or names an undeclared role — wording only (D-38). Tests: RoleTest (table and masks, serialization, intersection incl. read-only ceilings and shape bounds, Config overrides and violations).

#### P1.8.2 [M] `Layout` render `[S][R][K][T]` · DONE
- Why: [§5.1](docs/runtime/context-layout.md#sec-5-1) cache discipline: breakpoints at the ends of S/R/K/T; byte-stable per role/repo version/cell; no timestamps/counters in cached regions.
- Deps: P1.1.4, P1.3.2, P1.6.1, P0.3.2
- Build: `Layout.render(role, prime, k: CompiledK, t: Transcript) → List<Segment>`; `[S]` = kernel contract text ([Appendix A](docs/reference/kernel-contract.md#sec-appendix-a)) + role schemas + the three evidence lines + error policy + data/instruction rule + execution-mode label; `[K]` in S0 = contract slice + pre-existing ledger (seeds/notes/skills arrive P2–P4); `[T]` = native items, pinned user messages verbatim.
- Done: stability test: same inputs ⇒ identical segment bytes across turns; adapter `validate()` accepts.
- Log: 2026-09-21 — `cell.Layout` renders the four cached regions as `Segment`s, each closed by a breakpoint (four, within the provider's maximum). `Layout.system(role, mask, mode)` is `[S]`: the frozen `Kernel` contract (Appendix A, 14 numbered lines, `kernel/1`), the role's persona/duties/ask-back/packet, the seven families with an `enabled this turn` line (masked, never removed — the schemas themselves stay byte-stable in `Request.tools`), the three §4.3 evidence lines, the normative §5.4 `ErrorPolicy` table (`error-policy/1`), `Boundary.DATA_RULE` and `ExecutionModeLabel.render` — closing that P1.10.3/P1.10.4 integration debt. `[R]` is the prime text its compiler produced, `[K]` is `CompiledK` = contract slice verbatim + pre-existing ledger (seeds, notes, skills and carry-forward join the record in P2–P4, not here), `[T]` is `Transcript` = pinned user messages verbatim then the native items. Only `[S]` speaks as the system, so the data/instruction rule stays true of everything below it; a region the role's context view excludes, or that is empty, is omitted rather than sent empty. No clock, counter or absolute path enters a cached region: the whole render is a pure function of (role, mask, mode, prime, k, transcript). 7 tests: order and breakpoints, `[S]` content, what `[S]` does and does not vary with, byte stability across turns including an appended `[T]` leaving `[S][R][K]` identical, `[K]` with and without a ledger, omitted regions, and a full `Request` accepted by the fake adapter's `validate()`. Additive ABI; `@JvmStatic`/`@JvmOverloads` for Java callers.

#### P1.8.3 [M] `Anchor` render `[A]` · DONE
- Why: [§5.1 `[A]`](docs/runtime/context-layout.md#sec-5-1) ≤ 2.5K, rebuilt every turn, never cached; [§5.10 example](docs/reference/rendered-turn.md#sec-5-10).
- Build: `Anchor.render(digest ≤ 150, register ≤ 1.2K, workset ≤ 60, touched ≤ 10, checks ≤ 3 lines, focusZoom ≤ 300, focusNotes ≤ 300 (P4), gauge, nudges ≤ 2, fired trip lines (P1.5.2))` with `[A]` size metric.
- Done: golden test against §5.10; cap enforcement per block.
- Log: 2026-09-21 — `cell.Anchor.render` composes the volatile tail in the §5.10 order — contract digest, STATE, Workset, Touched, Checks, focus zoom, focus notes, gauge, nudges, fired trips — and returns `AnchorRender(text, tokens, overBudget, reductions)`, so the `[A]` size §6.8 calls first class is measured, not assumed, and `segment()` carries kind `A` with no breakpoint (rebuilt every turn, never cached). Each block is capped here as well as by its producer, because a caller can hand over an unbounded string and an oversized anchor is a cost bug that hides in every turn; truncation drops whole trailing lines and says how many. When the total still exceeds `anchorMaxTokens` the levers are pulled in a fixed, recorded order — focus notes, focus zoom, Touched down to three, STATE at half its cap, then the digest — while checks, the gauge, nudges and fired trips are never reduced, and a still-oversized anchor is reported rather than papered over. New: `Touched`/`TouchKind` (the §5.10 ledger line, built from a `VersionChange` by the cell loop) and at most two nudges per turn. 8 tests including the §5.10 golden render. `focusNotes` stays a parameter until P4 fills it.

#### P1.8.4 [M] `Gauge` on every result · DONE
- Build: gauge appended to every envelope (P1.6.1) and after the turn; inputs from residency (`ctx %`), reserve, checks state, Workset, register version, turn counter.
- Done: ~20 tokens; present on every result.
- Log: 2026-09-21 — `cell.Gauges` assembles the P1.6.1 `Gauge` from the components that own each number (L9): window occupancy from residency, the reserve from `CellBudget.Snapshot`, the summary from the checker, coverage from the `Workset`, the version from the `Register`, the turn from the loop. `contextPercent` is a parameter, not derived from the budget — budget spend and window occupancy answer different questions and conflating them is how a gauge starts lying; P1.8.6 supplies the real number and an out-of-range value fails fast. `Gauges.result(outcome, gauge)` is the `[T]` rendering `ToolOutcome.text` deliberately is not: the §5.4 envelope closed by the gauge, and a headerless refusal still carries it. A blank checks summary renders `none`, because "none" and "unknown" are different answers. 5 tests; the size test pins that only the checks summary varies, since §17's ~20 tokens is a real tokenizer's count and the byte heuristic over-counts this symbol-dense line.

#### P1.8.5 [M] `Gates` and nudges (S0 set) · DONE
- Why: [§5.6](docs/runtime/gates-termination.md#sec-5-6) one line each, once per condition, computed by the harness.
- Build: `Gates.evaluate(state) → List<Nudge|Rejection>`: entry (first non-register edit without an `accept:` step / unresolved acceptance), exit (P1.7.7), pressure (`tokens > α·C_max` ⇒ P1.8.7 handling), stall (3 turns without a progress event; live build output is work), loop (identical `(tool, args, result hash)` twice ⇒ nudge; third ends the turn with a required `state` op), no/two cursors, red-not-recorded, stale-fact-in-Next, reserve, turn budget 80 %. Scope/acceptance-surface (P3.4), impact (P3.2.4), contract touch (P3.4.3; active once CON notes exist), repeated failure signature (P4.6.2) register later through the same interface.
- Done: each gate fires once per condition in tests; progress events defined (evidence-backed tick, h→v with id, green run advancing an AC, verified new fact, new dead end).
- Log: 2026-09-21 — `cell.Gates` is a registered list of `Gate`s, each a pure function of one `GateState` record: no clock, no store, no model prose. The S0 set in §5.6 table order is entry, exit, pressure, stall, loop, register invariants (cursor / red-not-recorded / other validator rule), stale fact, reserve, turn budget. `Gates.s0().with(…)` is the seam P3.2.4, P3.4, P3.4.3 and P4.6.2 register through — names must be unique and a gate may only emit keys under its own name — and their inputs arrive as defaulted `GateState` fields (a test registers a sample impact gate through it). Once-per-condition is a `GateKey(gate, condition)` threaded back through `GateReport.fired`, with the condition encoded so a recurring condition re-fires while a persisting one does not (stall `since-<turn>`, pressure `rebuild-<n>`, loop `<tool>:<args8>/<result8>:<count>`). **Rejections are never deduplicated** — a refusal silenced the second time is a gate that opens — so for hard gates each attempt is the condition; that reading is stated in the code. The exit gate calls P1.7.7 rather than restating it. `Progress.events(before, after, turn, certifiedBefore, certifiedAfter)` defines the five progress events from records alone; a plan add, an `h` fact, a decision or a Next line is not progress. `Validator.riskFlags` moved unchanged into a public companion so the stale-fact gate shares the heuristic instead of copying it (instance behaviour untouched). 11 tests, one per gate plus progress and the seam; additive ABI. Delegated to a Fable worktree agent and merged with `--no-ff`. Deviations reported by the author and accepted: `evaluate` returns `GateReport(outcomes, fired)` rather than a bare list, because the once-per-condition memory has to leave a pure function; the stale-fact gate covers Next and the active step only, since a decision carries no fact reference to test against and a second word-overlap heuristic would be invented, not derived; `HypothesisVerified` is detected by normalized text match because the register has no h→v op. Left to P1.8.7: computing `lastProgressTurn` and `signatures`, acting on pressure (checkpoint + partial) and on the loop gate's `endsTurn`/`requiredOp`, and ordering the ≤ 2 nudges into `[A]`. `contextTokens`/`contextMaxTokens` come from P1.8.6.

#### P1.8.6 [M] `Residency`: eviction, stubs, recall · DONE
- Why: [§5.7](docs/runtime/residency-rebuild.md#sec-5-7); L1; F26 (batched eviction preserves the prefix cache).
- Build: `Residency(k = 8, rMax = 16K, stubTokens ≈ 20)`: results live `k` turns then stub; total live-result budget `R_max` stubs the oldest refetchable early; batch order by `p_reuse · c_refetch` (raw current-state observations first, verdicts last); model messages older than `3k` turns trimmed to first line + calls; user messages and packet pinned; complete tool-call/result units preserved (adapter `validate()` rejects broken pairs — FX-21); stub = recoverable pointer to captured bytes; `C(t)` accounting from §5.7.
- Done: residency bound holds with multi-result turns; one cache miss per `k` turns in the fake adapter's accounting.
- Log: 2026-09-21 — `cell.Residency(k, rMaxTokens, estimator)` over `Resident` records. **Eviction order:** candidates are live, unpinned, refetchable results of *earlier* turns (this turn's are exempt — a result the model has not read yet has no reuse to estimate), sorted by `(class rank, p_reuse·c_refetch, turn, position)`. The class rank Observation < Search < Verdict carries the dominant term of `c_refetch` — a verdict cannot be refetched, because the world it judged is gone — which is why §5.7 puts raw observations first and verdicts last; within a class `p_reuse = 1/(1+age)`, nil for a Workset-dropped body, and `c_refetch` is the body's tokens. **Batching (F26):** `due(residents, turn)` names `Age` on the `k` cadence, else `Budget` when the live total exceeds `R_max`; a batch applies the age rule, then the `3k` model-message trim (first line plus the calls, which are separate items), then the bound. A batch with nothing to do rewrites nothing, so the prefix survives. **Pairing (FX-21):** nothing is ever removed — a stub is a `ToolResult` with the same `callId`, a trim a `Message` in place, and the output list keeps the input's length and order; proved by `Items.pairs(…).broken == false` after every batch, by `FakeAdapter.validate == Ok` on the stubbed history, and by a negative control where dropping one result yields `Rejected(BrokenToolPairing)`. **Recoverability (invariant 9):** a stub renders `⟦stub #10 <label> · 1.1K tok · recall #10⟧` (10–30 tokens, no body bytes) over a `RecallPointer(alias, observationId, contentRef)` a test resolves back to the exact captured bytes; a pointerless body is never stubbed for the bound, is stubbed by age only with a recorded `Loss`, and unrecoverable overflow is reported as `overBound` rather than hidden. `Occupancy` is the §5.7 `C(t)`: prefix, live results, stubs, messages, anchor and new tokens, with `uncached`, `cached` and a clamped `percentOf(limit)` for `Gauges.of`. 11 residency tests; the cache measurement runs 40 turns × 2 results through the fake adapter and shows `[T]` cache-read dropping to zero exactly at requests 17, 25 and 33 — one miss per `k` turns — and equal to the whole previous `[T]` otherwise. Delegated to a Fable worktree agent and merged with `--no-ff`; the ABI conflict with P1.8.5 was resolved by regenerating the dump, and `checkKotlinAbi` passes with both features present.
- Log: 2026-09-21 (fixture) — the fake adapter's cache model was **corrected** as part of this task, not tuned to make the criterion pass: it billed a segment as a cache read only when the whole segment was byte-identical, so an append-only `[T]` was a full miss every turn and a prefix cache could never be observed. It now reads the unchanged leading items of a segment its previous breakpoint closed and pays for the tail only, and a rewrite before that boundary is a miss for that segment and everything after it — which is what a provider's prefix cache does and what makes F26's "batch, do not trickle" argument measurable at all. One `FakeAdapterTest` case added (11→12).
- Deviation (accepted): `Residency` measures each stub with the injected estimator instead of a `stubTokens ≈ 20` constant, because §17 declares no such number. Open implementation choice, recorded rather than silently decided: a `Budget` batch does **not** reset the `k` cadence, which keeps the age rule's bound at `2k−1` turns; revisit in P1.8.7 if the cadence proves too rigid under multi-result turns.

#### P1.8.7 [M] `Cell` turn loop · DONE
- Why: [§3.7 `cell()`](docs/architecture/lifecycle.md#sec-3-7) with F03 corrections; [§5.5](docs/runtime/tools.md#sec-5-5) transactional turns.
- Deps: P1.6.2, P1.8.2–P1.8.6, P1.7.2, P1.4.3, P0.3.4
- Build: `Cell.run(ctx, increment, budget): CellExit` — per turn: enforce dispatch authority + budget reservation; render anchor; `adapter.validate` + hard admission check (P2.3.3 refines) + `complete`; `validate_complete_calls_and_dependencies` (fail closed, no partial-call execution); journal native output **before** results; append native items (assistant calls precede results); no calls ⇒ role completion path (P1.8.8); partition + dispatch; reconcile workspace + persist checkpoint on **every** exit path; end-of-turn checker; terminal request handling; eviction every `k`; pressure: in P1 checkpoint + terminate `partial` with a replan hint (full rebuild is P2.5.2; a P1 cell never summarises); turn budget exhaustion ⇒ `partial`.
- Done: first vertical slice (P1.12.2) runs through this loop; every exit path leaves a persisted checkpoint (fault injection).
- Log: 2026-09-21 — `Cell.run(ctx, increment, budget): CellExit` in `cell/Cell.kt`, with `CellContext` (+`CellModel`/`CellTools`/`CellWorkspace`/`CellEvidence`), `CellExit`/`PartialReason` and `Checkpoints` (`CellCheckpoint`, `CellStatus`; in-memory plus a SQLite writer of `cells`/`turns`/`workset_exports` in one transaction). **Turn phases:** authority + `startTurn` → render `[A]` and `[S][R][K][T]` → `adapter.validate` + admission → `complete` → journal the native output → append items → validate the whole turn's calls → partition and dispatch → reconcile (stamp diff and announcements, checks refresh, drain scheduled checker/atlas, workset drops, eviction, occupancy) → gates → checkpoint. **Exits:** `Completed`, `Blocked`, `Partial(Pressure|TurnBudget|TokenBudget|Reserve|CompletionStalled)`, `Failed`, `Cancelled` — distinct, never disguised as completion (invariant 11). **Checkpoint on every path:** one `settle()` runs for the normal finish, for an exception and for cancellation (under `NonCancellable`, then rethrowing), reconciling first when the turn had not; proved under fault injection with a poll that throws mid-run (`unknown_outcome` with the intent left open in every checkpoint), a crash at the postimage put before the registry hears (the boundary reconcile announces and drops the stale read), a crash at the result-body put after the announcement (unseen post-edit coverage dropped) and a crash in the run's persist (the intent stays `Unknown` through the exit). **Fail closed:** an invalid parse, a rejected partition, a missing required `state` op after the loop gate or an edit on a reserve turn gives every native call an error `ToolResult` naming the reason and dispatches nothing, so the call/result pairing stays complete. **Integration debts closed** (§1 resume notes): the coherence order Workset → register → Checks with the Touched ledger; scheduled checker and atlas refreshes drained; checks refreshed after stamps; `CellBudget` admission with a usage reconcile; run/edit/unannounced-move `TestIntegrity` flags passed to the exit gate; preimages journaled. 14 cell tests plus a `CellTesting` fixture that wires the whole S0 runtime with an armed crash point; full `:core:test` **935 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors, 6 existing platform skips**. Delegated to a Fable worktree agent and merged `--no-ff`.
- Log: 2026-09-21 (integration finding, fixed here) — the exit gate treated **every** non-green receipt as red, so an end-of-turn check that came back *inconclusive* — which every generic shaper does until P3.1.4 gives it a diagnostics parser — blocked every completion. `Currency` gains `red` (the receipt's outcome is `failed`), defaulting to `!green` for callers that predate the distinction, and §8.7's "red without an Open item" rule uses it. **Acceptance is not weakened:** a required acceptance id still needs `certifies` (current, eligible and green), a signed review or an accepted assessment, and an inconclusive receipt certifies nothing. The change only stops an *unknown* outcome masquerading as a failure, which L8 forbids. Recorded as a specification refinement in §3.1. ABI note: `Currency` gained a field, so its `copy` signature changed — the only non-additive entry in this dump; `@JvmOverloads` keeps the previous constructor.
- Open finding for P1.12.2 (not a blocker): recall pointers exist only for `look` results. A `run` or `edit` alias resolves to an action or edit id rather than an observation, so `Look.recall` cannot serve it; a stubbed run body is therefore recorded as a loss rather than recalled. Decide in the vertical slice whether run/edit captures earn observation ids.
- Deviations (accepted, author-reported): `CellExit.turns` counts `budget.turnsTaken`, so a turn refused before dispatch is not counted although its checkpoint row is written; the per-turn read budget is `R_max`; the reserve-turn edit refusal is whole-turn, consistent with fail-closed, because the dispatcher cannot skip a single call. Seams left open: `DispatchAuthority` (lease/cancellation, P1.9) and `RoleCompletion`/`RoleOutput`/`CompletionDecision` (P1.8.8).

#### P1.8.8 [M] Role completion and `ResultPacket` · DONE
- Why: [§5.9](docs/runtime/gates-termination.md#sec-5-9); `assess_role_completion` (implementing gate vs declared packet validators).
- Build: `ResultPacket` with all §5.9 fields (`contract_version`, `execution_generation`, `base`, `read_versions` runtime-observed, `status` proposal, `waiting`, `register`, `workset_export`, `changes`, `transforms`, `receipts` refs, `stamp`, `coverage` from telemetry, `flags`, `not_tested`, `notes_to_persist`, `open_questions`, `blocked`, `self_assessment`, `cost`); `Completion.assess(proposal, role)` (implementing ⇒ `ExitGate`; other roles ⇒ packet validators in P2/P4); gaps recorded; `cannot_progress` ⇒ incomplete exit; a completion proposal may request scheduler-owned checks but is accepted only with current evidence (outer verification reuses them).
- Done: packet fields never taken from model text where the docs say runtime-owned; `done` is a proposal until the verifier accepts.
- Log: 2026-09-24 — `cell.ResultPacket` carries every §5.9 field and is on **every** `CellExit` (`packet`), built by the loop from records and journaled as one `Boundary` event (`packet <status> · n changes · n receipts · stamp`) before the exit returns (§3.7 `persist_role_packet`). **Runtime-owned, never model text:** `status` = `PacketStatus.of(CellStatus)` (`Done` only when the completion seam accepted; `Replan`/`Waiting` are declared vocabulary with no P1 producer); `contract_version` = the version the last turn ran under; `execution_generation` = new `CellContext.generation` (`ExecutionGeneration`, default `INITIAL`; P1.9.4 supplies the real one); `base` = the stamp taken before turn 1 + workspace id; `read_versions` = every path's last *displayed* version from the Workset (fully redacted views are not reads, D-49); `changes` = each path's first→last registry transition (the Touched ledger), net no-ops dropped, `ChangeOrigin` Edit | Run from the executing family, External from the boundary reconcile's unannounced moves (an agent origin supersedes External); `receipts` = each check's latest receipt id; `stamp`/`envId` = the final reconcile; `coverage` = displayed ranges per (path, version) and the cell's own changes it was never shown; `flags` = changes outside the increment's write scope + the collected `TestIntegrity` flags; `cost` = usage summed per billable dimension with calls, calls without usage (a provider error or an all-unknown usage record counts, never estimated), unreported dimensions and dispatch seconds by the injected clock. The model reaches only `claims`: S0 fills `openQuestions` from STATE's unclosed Open items and unanswered `task.ask` questions; `not_tested` (P3.1), note candidates (P4.2) and the self-assessment (P2.1.4) have no S0 producer. `ResultPacket.proposal(patchHash?)` is the verifier's `CompletionProposal` (base, final stamp, env, contract version); a `Failed` packet refuses — recovery, never verification. **Completion:** `RoleCompletion.forRole(role, validators, maxFinalizations)` is `Completion.assess`: a registered validator for the role's `PacketKind`, else the P1.7.7 exit gate for the implementing `Result` packet, else `CannotProgress` naming the missing validator — never a fallback to the implementing gate. `Cell(completion = null)` resolves it from the context's role; `RoleOutput` now carries the provisional `Done` packet validators judge. Refused proposals are recorded gaps: journaled (`Nudge`, `completion refused at turn n`) and carried in `packet.gaps`. A proposal requests scheduler-owned checks in S0 through `verify` calls; it is accepted only on current receipts, which the packet references for the outer verification to reuse. Tests: ResultPacketTest (6) — a completed packet whose final text claims another file, green tests and a status word, with every runtime field checked against the stores, `proposal()` accepted by `Verifier` (R1 verified) and refused once the tree moves; a blocked packet with an out-of-scope edit, an external mid-cell edit, a test-integrity flag and an open question; probe without a validator ⇒ `Partial(CompletionStalled)` with the gap; a registered `Investigation` validator sees the provisional packet; implementing gaps recorded across two refusals; a failed packet refuses `proposal()`. Local verification (Linux sandbox, JDK 25 scratch copy — see resume notes): cell 65/65; full core 941 tests / 3 failures / 6 skips, the three all outside the change and reproduced on the unmodified baseline or in isolation (`FixtureReposTest` gradle-small and `SearchBackendParityTest` café fail identically at `00d0db6` in this sandbox; `EventsTest` passes in isolation). **ABI (non-additive, reviewed):** the five `CellExit` constructors/`copy` gain `packet`; `CellContext`'s full constructor gains `generation` (`@JvmOverloads` keeps the earlier overloads); `Cell`'s `completion` is nullable; `RoleOutput` gains `packet`. Deviations: no `diffstat` in `Change` — the packet carries versions, and P1.9.5 derives line counts from preimages for the finish receipt; `transforms` is always empty until P3.3; `waiting` is always `null` until P2 background handles.

### P1.9 Controller (S0 path) and P0 lifecycle controls
#### P1.9.1 [C][M] `Lifecycle` state machines · DONE
- Why: [§3.2](docs/architecture/components.md#sec-3-2) "one state machine over typed records"; [§5.9 outcomes](docs/runtime/gates-termination.md#sec-5-9); invariant 11.
- Pkg: `campaign`
- Build: `CampaignState`/`CampaignOutcome { completed, waiting_for_process, waiting_for_input, blocked_external, budget_exhausted, cancelled, failed }`, `CellState`, `IncrementStatus`, typed transitions (only via receipts/packets), persisted with the ledger; `partial` is never `verified`.
- Done: illegal transitions impossible by construction; outcome table exhaustively tested.
- Log: 2026-09-24 — `campaign/Lifecycle.kt`: `CampaignOutcome` (the seven §5.9 outcomes, `wire` spelling, `resumable` = waiting_for_process · waiting_for_input · blocked_external), `CampaignPhase { Opened, Running, Finishing, Ended }`, `CellState(cell, increment, CellStatus)` (the existing `cell.CellStatus` is the cell machine; no second enum) and `CampaignState` (work, attempt, contract version, phase, `RequirementGraph`, `Ledger`, cells, outcome, reason, `seq`). **Illegal states are unconstructible:** `CampaignState` has a private constructor and `@ConsistentCopyVisibility`, so a state comes only from `Lifecycle.open(contract, graph)` (graph must validate) or `Lifecycle.apply(state, contract, transition)`; its `init` rejects an outcome without `Ended`, `completed` over an unfinished ledger and two running cells. **Typed transitions, each carrying its record:** `Reconciled(unknownOutcomes)` Opened→Running; `Dispatched(increment, cell)` via `graph.continueIncrement`, one running cell; `Returned(exit)` must be the running cell's packet, a `Blocked` exit parks the increment (new `RequirementGraph.block(id, cell)`); `Committed(Accepted, stampNow)` only for the increment's **latest cell with status `Completed`** and a result about the tree now, via `graph.recordAccepted`, ledger recomputed with `graph.ledger(contract, stamp)` — so partial/blocked/failed/cancelled cells and superseded or stale completions never verify; `Unblocked(increment, authorityRef)` (new `RequirementGraph.unblock`); `IncrementCancelled`; `Finishing(stamp)`/`Finished(stamp, receipts≠∅)` require every requirement `stampValid` at that stamp and no open increment — the only path to `completed`; `Stopped(outcome≠Completed, reason)` requires no running cell (reconcile first); `Resumed` only from a resumable outcome, back to `Opened`. Contract must be the campaign's work/attempt, version non-decreasing. **Outcome table** `Lifecycle.disposition(exit, completion)` → `Close(accepted)` · `Continue(reason, fallback)` · `Stop(outcome, reason)`: completed+accepted ⇒ close; completed+refused ⇒ continue (fallback failed), recovery-directed ⇒ stop failed; blocked with question ⇒ waiting_for_input, without ⇒ blocked_external; partial ⇒ continue with fallback budget_exhausted (turn/token/reserve) or failed (pressure/stalled) (D-64); failed/cancelled ⇒ stop; an `Accepted` result for any non-completed exit is rejected. **Persistence** `campaign/Campaigns.kt`: `Campaigns` seam, `InMemoryCampaigns`, `SqliteCampaigns` writing the `campaigns` row plus the `increments` and `ledger` projections `Views.ledger` reads in one transaction; a save must extend the stored `seq` by exactly one (`StaleCampaignState`), so a replay or a second writer is refused. **Schema v2** (`Migrations.SCHEMA_VERSION = 2`): `campaigns (ids, phase, outcome, seq, meta, PK(work_id, attempt_id))`, controller-owned. 9 `LifecycleTest` tests incl. the full phase × transition legality table and every exit × partial reason × verifier result; graph/store suites green (71); full core 950 tests / 2 known environmental failures / 6 skips on the JDK 25 sandbox copy. ABI purely additive (reviewed), `checkKotlinAbi` green. Left to owners: `Transition` producers (P1.9.2 reconcile/open, P1.9.3 dispatch/commit/finish, P1.9.4 cancellation/lease generation checks), `waiting_for_process` has no producer until P2 handles.

#### P1.9.2 [M] Campaign open and reconciliation · DONE
- Why: [§3.7 `campaign()`](docs/architecture/lifecycle.md#sec-3-7) first lines; [§13.1](docs/operations/recovery.md#sec-13-1) reconcile before any consequential action.

- Build: `Controller.open(repo, request, policy)`: create/load contract, workspace, store, `EmptyKb`; capture dirty state + `s0`; reconcile open intents and stamp drift (`Touched (external)`); impact pre-scan stub returns `unknown` until P3.2.6; `ShapeSelector` in P1 returns `S0` when eligible, else `blocked("shape S1+ unavailable")` (honest, FX-48/49).
- Done: reopening a store with an open intent yields `unknown_outcome` and blocks retries (FX-23 open-time part).
- Log: 2026-09-24 — `campaign/Controller.kt`: `Controller.open(repo, CampaignRequest, CampaignPolicy)` → `OpenedCampaign` (owns store + project lock + `LocalOs`; `advance(transition)` applies and saves, one writer). Order: store → workspace (`ProtectedPaths` bound to workspace and contract scope) → dirty capture = shadow snapshot 0 on first open (reopen reads it back) → `deriveS0` stored after the capture → `Checks.seed` with the sniffed primary package's `RunnerCommands` → approved rules into `Prime` → campaign load / `Lifecycle.open(G_single)` / `Resumed` for a resumable end → reconcile: uncommitted intents ⇒ `Unknown` + `Reconcile` journal event + `run.reconciled`, tree drift vs the last shadow snapshot ⇒ `Touched(…, note="external")` + journal event + next snapshot → `Transition.Reconciled(unknownIds)` → `Prescan.UNKNOWN` → `campaign/ShapeSelector.kt` (`select`, `single` = `G_single(C)` with `produces=Artifact`) → S1+ ⇒ `Stopped(blocked_external, "shape S1+ unavailable: …")` (D-65). Retry block: `Intent.replaySafe`, `Run` refuses a relaunch of an unknown non-safe intent's argv+cwd (`unknown_outcome`). Integration debt paid: `Git.rootCommits` excludes `refs/astrolabe/*` — the parentless shadow snapshot 0 otherwise changed `RepoIdentity` and moved the store on reopen. 5 `ControllerTest` tests (fresh open, FX-23 open-time with refused relaunch, external drift, blocked-then-resumed, no suite ⇒ `waiting_for_input`). Limits: a reopen in phase `Running` (crash mid-cell) keeps the stored state — settling the running cell is P2.2.4; debt 2 (`G_single` validation) is now paid here for `run:` contracts.

- Why: [§3.6](docs/architecture/lifecycle.md#sec-3-6) data flow for one increment; `G_single(C)`.

Debt 2 paid. Limits: full suite not run at finish (P1.9.5); cancellation/leases/`AttemptConfig` are P1.9.4; trace extraction P4.

#### P1.9.4 [M] P0 lifecycle controls · DONE
- Why: [§3.5](docs/architecture/roles-shapes.md#sec-3-5) last row (F28): cancellation, leases, budgets, reconciliation, accounting active in every shape; F09.
- Deps: P0.1.3, P0.2.2, P0.5.1, P1.11.2
- Build: `AttemptConfig` frozen at campaign open (harness version, validated config snapshot incl. flags and role-text versions, profiles) — invariant 12 applies to every attempt, so mid-attempt configuration changes are ignored and a production config that disables a mandatory control is rejected (D-48, I-02); `ProjectLock` acquired before any consequential action (D-44); `Cancellation` token checked before dispatch and before any publication, with bounded cleanup of reservations and evidence that never discards late effects (D-26); `Lease(workspace, holder, expiry, executionGeneration)` single writer per workspace, expiry revokes publication authority only; `Reservations` per cell (atomic, P0.2.2); reconciliation on every terminal path; `Accounting` per call (P1.11.2).
- Done: FX-25, FX-48, FX-49 (S0 parameterization); cancelled cell's late effects are archived, its publication refused (single-cell form of FX-26); a config edit during an attempt has no effect until the next attempt (IX-02, IX-18).
- Log: 2026-09-24 — `campaign/Controls.kt`: `Cancellation` token (first reason wins; listeners interrupt the in-flight cell), `Lease(workspace, holder, expiry, generation)` + `Leases` over the `leases` table (one live holder; reassignment increments the execution generation; `authority(lease)` refuses when superseded or expired — expiry revokes publication only), `Attempts` over the new **schema v3** `attempts` table (frozen `AttemptConfig` per work/attempt). Controller: the first open freezes `AttemptConfig.freeze(config)` (production validation, D-48; an invalid config never starts), every reopen reads it back and warns (`config-frozen`) when the live config differs — derivation, rules, shape, compile, cell and tools all use the frozen config (IX-02/18); the lease is taken after open-time reconciliation (§13.1); `OpenedCampaign.refusal()` (cancellation, then lease) gates dispatch (`DispatchAuthority` for the cell, and before the first dispatch) and publication (before `Committed` and before `Finished`); a refused late completion is journalled `late completion … archived; publication refused` and stops `cancelled` (or `blocked_external` on a lost lease) with the effect left on disk (single-cell FX-26). The cell runs in a child job the token cancels mid-call; the settled `Cancelled` checkpoint enters the new typed `Transition.Interrupted(checkpoint)`; a cell that finished before the cancellation keeps its exit. `CampaignHandle.cancel()` now cancels through the token. `ProjectLock` (store open) precedes everything; reservations are the cell budget's (P0.2.2); accounting per call is P1.11.2. 8 `ControlsTest` tests (IX-02/18, invalid config, cancel before dispatch, cancel mid-call, FX-26, lease single-writer + expiry, FX-25 S0 budget, FX-48/49 S0 with overhead measured: 4 turns, 4 priced calls). Test configs now name their profiles (the freeze validates them).

#### P1.9.6 [C][M] `Astrolabe` facade and `AstrolabeJava` · DONE

- Deps: P1.9.3, P0.1.3, P0.4.1–P0.4.3
- Pkg: root `io.astrolabe`, `java`
- Build: `Astrolabe(config, adapter: ProviderAdapter, authority: Authority)`: `open(repo): Project` (state root, project lock, workspace, store, kb), `suspend campaign(project, request, policy?): CampaignHandle` (`workId`, `await(): CampaignOutcome`, `cancel()`, `amend(text)`, `events: Flow<AgentEvent>`, `views`), `close()` (resource closure documented); `AstrolabeJava` mirrors it with `CompletableFuture` (cancellation propagates to the campaign), blocking variants, `EventSink` registration, and accepts `JavaProviderAdapter`/`JavaAuthority` implementations through the bridges of P0.3.4/P0.4.2; documented exception mapping and callback threading; no `suspend`, `Flow` or `value class` in `io.astrolabe.java` (D-07, I-14).
- Done: P1.12.3 uses only `AstrolabeJava` with Java-authored adapter and authority; ABI dump reviewed.
- Log: 2026-09-24 — `Astrolabe.kt`: `Astrolabe(config, adapter, authority, clock, idGen)` (validates `Config`, owns `events` and a supervisor scope; constants move to the companion: `MODULE`, `VERSION`, `FIRST_ATTEMPT`), `open(repo): Project` (store + project lock + `LocalOs` + `views` + `EmptyKb`; host-closed), `suspend campaign(project, request, policy?)` → `CampaignHandle` (`workId`, `await()` = outcome or `cancelled`, `cancel()`, `amend(text)`, `events: Flow<AgentEvent>` filtered by work, `views`, `done`); one campaign per project at a time; profile = `config.profiles[profileRoles.main]`; D-67 default budget. `Controller.open(project, …)` overload leaves store/OS to the project (`OpenedCampaign.owned`). `java/AstrolabeJava.kt`: `AstrolabeJava(config, JavaProviderAdapter, JavaAuthority)` via `ProviderAdapters.fromJava`/`Authorities.fromJava`; `campaign` → `CompletableFuture<JavaCampaignHandle>`, `campaignBlocking`, `subscribe(EventSink)`; `JavaCampaignHandle` (`await()` future whose cancellation cancels the campaign, `awaitBlocking`, `cancel`, `amend`, `views`, per-campaign `subscribe`); threading, exception mapping and cancellation documented in KDoc; no `suspend`/`Flow`/value class in `io.astrolabe.java`. `JavaConsumerSmoke` reads `Astrolabe.MODULE`/`VERSION`. 3 `AstrolabeTest` tests (run + events + honest outcome; cancel ⇒ `cancelled`, amend, second campaign refused; Java SPI adapter/authority through futures with a per-campaign sink). ABI dump regenerated at the gate; the Java-source smoke is P1.12.3.

### P1.10 Authority and integrity baseline
#### P1.10.1 [M] `Boundary`: delimiters and instruction-shape flag · DONE
- Why: [§14.3](docs/platform/security.md#sec-14-3); F11; delimiters are cues, not security.
- Pkg: `auth`
- Build: escape of delimiter-like payload bytes; `InstructionShape.detect(text)` heuristics ⇒ envelope flag `⚠ instruction-shaped content` (never filtered, never executed); `RulesTrust` (D-32): discovery proposes candidate files; only a binding from host configuration or explicit user authority (canonical path + digest + provenance) promotes a snapshot to instruction text; the approved snapshot is reused across resumes; replaced bytes drop back to data until re-approved; editing the rules path never elevates edited bytes; `[S]` data/instruction rule text.
- Done: FX-38 (repo file / tool result instructing the agent ⇒ data, flagged, authorization unchanged); an untrusted rules-named file stays data, a trusted snapshot loads, replacement + resume cannot elevate new instructions (IX-08).
- Log: 2026-09-20 — auth (delegated worktree agent, merged): InstructionShape.detect (8 weighted signals, threshold 3, FLAG '⚠ instruction-shaped content'; a cue, never a filter), Boundary.present(payload) = escape + flag; RulesTrust(workspaceRoot): discover (.astrolabe/rules.md, AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md as data), bind(candidate, Provenance HostConfig|UserAuthority) → RulesBinding (pure, records the digest shown to the authority), status/approved ⇒ Untrusted | Missing | Unreadable | ChangedSinceApproval | Approved(snapshot); replaced bytes never inherit approval, resume reuses the binding (IX-08, FX-38). Consumers not yet wired: [S] render (P1.8.2), look/recall/export redaction (P1.6.3).

#### P1.10.2 [M] Capability ceiling and effect policy · DONE
- Why: L10; [§14.1](docs/platform/security.md#sec-14-1); executor enforces regardless of model request.
- Build: `Capability`/`Ceiling` from `Contract.authorization.capabilitySet`; `Policy.classify(run) → EffectClass` (protected paths ⇒ D; network/outside-workspace/git refs/package install (configurable)/privilege/destructive git ⇒ D); generated scripts inherit the caller's ceiling; `ExecutionMode { TrustedLocal, Confined }` label in `[S]` and every report (the label describes limitations, it enforces nothing); a host that *requires* `Confined` fails at configuration/dispatch until a backend exists (P7) — trusted-local is never substituted (D-11).
- Done: a masked/denied op is refused in the executor even when the schema would allow it; FX-39 (ceiling) single-executor form; `Confined` required + no backend ⇒ refusal, not a relabelled trusted-local run.
- Log: 2026-09-20 — Capability (8) / CapabilitySet (workspace-local-test-only, workspace-read-only, host sets), Ceiling.of(authorization, executionMode) with allows(op, mask) → Refusal (masked/unknown/missing capability/stage) — refuses even when the schema lists the op (FX-39 single-executor); EffectPolicy.classify(argv|RunArgs, cwd, workspaceRoot, protectedPaths, config) → Classification(effectClass, reasons, requiredCapabilities, effectsUnknown, approximate) with a configurable D-class table (privilege, network, package install, git ref mutation, destructive deletes outside tmp, any argument outside the workspace or under a protected path), W for known builders/formatters/runners, R otherwise (label only; stamp diff reclassifies in P1.6.5); shell cmd segments classified individually (most restrictive wins, approximate); ExecutionModeLabel + Executors.require(mode, backends) ⇒ Refused when Confined is required without a backend (D-11).

#### P1.10.3 [M] `Redaction` · DONE
- Build: `Redaction.apply(bytes) → (redacted, mask: source-line ↔ rendered-line mapping, limitations)` before model exposure and before reusable evidence persistence; the mask travels with the `Observation` and excludes redacted lines from coverage (D-49); recall and export never return unredacted bytes; signed opaque provider items are stored in protected `native/` and never rewritten (D-25); env allowlist in runner; recovery preimages exempt and access-restricted (D-14); `capture.redacted` and redaction limits recorded per observation.
- Done: secret patterns never reach envelopes, notes, recall or exports in tests; a redacted line inside a multi-line anchor grants no coverage while exact unredacted spans remain usable (IX-10); preimage revert still exact.
- Log: 2026-09-20 — Redaction(config): RedactionConfig(patterns ×9 defaults incl. private-key blocks, AWS/GitHub/OpenAI/Slack tokens, JWT, bearer, URL credentials, secret assignments; envAllowlist; maxBytes 256 KiB) validated through Config.violations(); apply/applyBytes → Redacted(text, RedactionMask hiddenLines, hits, limitations) preserving line count so the mask is line-exact (IX-10 coverage test); byte cap cuts at a line boundary and records a limitation, never returns the tail; ContentClass ModelFacing|ReusableEvidence|NativeReplay|RecoveryPreimage with refuse()/neverRewrite() guarding native replay items (D-25) and recovery preimages (D-14). Config gained the redaction section.

#### P1.10.4 [C] `PermissionLadder` data model · DONE
- Why: [§14.2](docs/platform/security.md#sec-14-2) separate grants `patch → local commit → push → merge → deploy`; ceiling from the contract.
- Build: `Stage` enum, `PermissionLadder(ceiling)`, `highestAuthorizedStage` tracking; only `patch` reachable in P1 (commit policy P5.2).
- Done: any attempt above the ceiling is refused and recorded.
- Log: 2026-09-20 — PermissionLadder(ceiling, reachable = {Patch}): request(stage) → Granted | Refused (above ceiling or not yet implemented), record(stage) monotone highestAuthorizedStage, refusals kept for the finish receipt; only Patch reachable in P1 (commit policy P5.2).

New event family `AgentEvent.Telemetry`: `span.started`, `span.ended` (cost `<cur> <amount>` or null, duration). `telemetry/Metrics.kt`: `CellMetrics.of(cell, records, layerOf)` (tokens by billing dimension — a dimension missing from any call is null —, model/tool calls, tool worker-seconds from `tool.called`→`tool.resulted`, checks by layer, gates, rebuilds, turns, boundary reason; `[A]`/STATE upkeep/manifest/pre-compilation null until P2.3), `CampaignMetrics.of(work, records, cost)` (cost per accepted task undefined at zero/unknown, first-attempt pass rate, verified/blocked/cancelled, continuations per increment, rebuilds per cell, unclassified interventions from questions; probes/reviews/escalations/boundary share null until P2.4/P2.5/P4.6), `ProjectMetrics` model (sources P2.6/P4.1/P4.5) and the `Outcomes.report(workId, outcome)` seam (`InMemoryOutcomes`, `DeploymentOutcomeKind`). Controller wiring: `Controller(…, spans)` wraps a campaign run (`plan`) and each cell (`edit`); `Astrolabe` passes one. 5 `SpansTest` tests; `AstrolabeTest` asserts the campaign span. #### P1.12.2 [V] First vertical slice · DONE
- Why: [§18.2 first vertical slice](docs/implementation/roadmap.md#sec-18-2).

- Done: slice passes; per-call billed usage visible in exports; turn counts recorded as the fake-adapter baseline (B0/B-HELM are live comparators, D-28).
- Log: 2026-09-24 — `campaign/VerticalSliceTest.kt`: cross-file defect (`calc.total` → `helper.scale`), a user-dirty `NOTES.md`, a stored contract with a counted `run:` item; cell 1 reads both files, sends one guarded patch against a stale version (refused), patches the current one, starts the test in the background (`handle-1`) and polls it, then the controller dies (`ProcessDeath`, an `Error` no layer swallows, injected at the next blob fsync) between the edit and the acceptance receipt. Reopen reconciles: the running cell becomes `lost` through the new typed `Transition.Lost(cell, lastCheckpoint)` (journalled), the unreceipted edit comes back as an external touch from the tree; cell 2 verifies the candidate and the campaign completes with a finish receipt that keeps `NOTES.md` untouched under `pre_existing_user_changes`, AC-1 green, and `usage.json` with billed usage for all 8 calls. Finish receipts now credit a packet-less (lost/interrupted) cell's checkpoint `touched` paths to `agent`. **Fake-adapter baseline:** 6 model calls before the death (5 settled turns), 2 turns after the reopen. Test hygiene: `runBlocking<Unit>` for every blocking test (a non-Unit body makes JUnit skip the method).

## P2 Stage B — Continuity (S1)
Goal: [§18.2 Stage B](docs/implementation/roadmap.md#sec-18-2): campaign controller, requirement graph, plan cell, increments, compiler with seeds/coverage/manifest, carry-forward, cross-cell coherence, the one rebuild mechanism (five uses), resume/reconcile, calibration prior, KB read path with STATUS/CAL notes written deterministically by the harness.
**Fixtures:** FX-11, 19, 20, 22, 23 (full resume), 35 (invalidation marks; injection exclusion P4), 42, 45, 46, 49 (S1), 51 (context-local coverage), 56, 57; IX-03 (human-review substitution), IX-06, IX-11, IX-17 + the four crash intervals of [§13.4](docs/operations/recovery.md#sec-13-4) · **Unsupported until later:** closures beyond `known(paths)`/reuse proofs (P3), impact pre-scan (P3.2.6), review cells (P4), KB admission/injection ranking (P4), probes/routing/recovery ladder (P4), S3 (P5). Live gate (B2 ≥ B1 on medium tasks): `UNMEASURED`.

### P2.1 Full contract, requirement graph, ledger, plan cell
#### P2.1.1 [C][M] `RequirementGraph`, `Increment`, `Ledger` · DONE
- Why: [§4.2](docs/state/contracts.md#sec-4-2); invariant 2; F5.

- Done: validation rejects "think more" nodes and uncovered requirements; frontier determinism test; FX-42 (verified increment never re-executed; regression obligation only).
- Log: 2026-09-20 — selected out of order by owner authorization (§1.2), baseline `d0ca86a`; existing contract/verification types retained. Implementation pending; normal queue resumes at reopened P0 validation when this slice closes.
- Log: 2026-09-20 — completed component in `graph/{Planning,Dependencies,RequirementGraph,GraphSerializer}.kt`, additive `contract.Increment` fields and verifier identity/evidence bindings; D-54. Iterative Tarjan SCC, longest-path Kahn depth, stable depth/id frontier, requirement coverage/prerequisite validation, immutable snapshots/serialization, continuation/cancellation history and exact-stamp derived ledger. FX-42, 100 random graphs against independent reachability, 20k-node depth, replay/aliasing/ledger regressions: 18 graph tests; one added verifier stale-review regression. Focused 44 passed; final full Windows build core 777 tests (zero failures/errors, six existing platform skips); provider-api 15 cached green results; ABI checks pass. Integration stays in P2.1.2/P2.1.4/P2.2.2/P2.2.4; no phase/Linux/live promotion claim. [Full log and return point](audit/OUT-OF-ORDER-P2.1.1.md).

#### P2.1.2 [M] Plan cell role and packet validator · DONE
- Why: [§3.4 plan cell](docs/architecture/roles-shapes.md#sec-3-4), [§4.1 origins](docs/state/contracts.md#sec-4-1), [§4.2](docs/state/contracts.md#sec-4-2); main line only, never a child.
- Deps: P2.1.1, P1.8.1, P0.4.2

### P2.2 Campaign controller (S1 path)
#### P2.2.1 [M] `ShapeSelector` (S0–S2) and activation table · DONE
- Why: [§3.5](docs/architecture/roles-shapes.md#sec-3-5) deterministic, logged `select_shape`; collapsibility contract; F09 (S3 only after validated planning, P5.1.4).
- Pkg: `campaign`
- Build: `ShapeSelector.select(contract, impact, plan?) → Shape + inputs log` (size classes D-16; risk = max(blast radius, contract touch ⇒ high, reversibility); S0 iff size S ∧ low risk ∧ no `review:` ∧ no resume expected; S2 iff `review:` items ∨ risk ≥ high ∨ contract touch ∨ ambiguous bug (D-39); else S1; S3 branch disabled until P5.1.4; unknown impact/discovery coverage is `unknown`, never low risk, and cannot establish S0 eligibility by itself (I-23)), `Activation(shape)` = feature set per the §3.5 table (used to mask tools/features); **required capabilities are selected independently of which automatic roles exist** (I-03, D-23): an S2 selection whose only missing capability is *required review* runs sequentially with `Authority.review` satisfying it, the substitution recorded in the shape log; a selection needing another unavailable capability (probes, routing, repair) ends as an honest `blocked("capability unavailable: …")`; upgrade only on traced evidence (pressure, probe request, risk floor); downgrade aggressively, but a downgrade never removes an outstanding required check/review; required review obligations (incl. refactor mode) select ≥ S2 or an authorized human review.
- Done: table-driven tests for every row of the shape table; `Campaign.ShapeSelected` event carries inputs.
- Log: 2026-09-24 — `ShapeSelector.select(contract, prescan, policy, resumeExpected, ambiguousBug, capabilities)`: `ShapeInputs` (requirements, files, packages, cross-package, D-16 `SizeClass` or unknown, `RiskLevel` = max(blast radius vs small/large file bounds, contract touch ⇒ high, hard reversibility ⇒ high) or `Unknown`, review items, resume, D-39 ambiguous bug) with a deterministic `log` line carried by `campaign.shape_selected`; S0 per D-65 (positive small facts, unknown risk/files only as limitations — I-23), else S2 for review/high risk/contract touch/ambiguous bug, else S1; S3 never initially. `ShapeCapabilities`: required review met by review cells or `Authority.review` (recorded substitution, D-23), ambiguous bug needs probes; missing ⇒ `Unavailable("capability unavailable: …")`. `adjust()` upgrades only on traced evidence and never downgrades below S2 with outstanding reviews. The feature mask per shape stays `Roles.shapeMask` (P1.8.1). The controller still runs S0 only: other selections are reported `shape S1+ unavailable … (campaign loop P2.2.2)`. `ShapeSelectorTest`: 13 table rows, unknown-impact logging, substitution/blocking, adjust.

#### P2.2.2 [M] Campaign loop · DONE
- Why: [§3.7 `campaign()`](docs/architecture/lifecycle.md#sec-3-7) verbatim structure, [§3.6](docs/architecture/lifecycle.md#sec-3-6).
- Deps: P2.1.*, P2.3, P2.4, P2.5
- Build: `Controller.run()`: `while unfinished_requirements`: enforce cancellation/leases/authority/reservations → incorporate observations + invalidate (Coherence) → stop/external wait ⇒ persist honest outcome → `next_ready(budget)`; empty frontier ⇒ wait/block/exhaustion (never `completed`) → handle-wait polling or independent work → `select_profile` (single main profile until P4.5) → `take_valid_precompiled_or_compile` (pre-compilation P3.7) → capacity/evidence gap handled without dispatch (`NEEDS_RESCOPING` ⇒ ask the plan role for `increment_split`; `NEEDS_MORE_EVIDENCE` ⇒ worker investigates) → cell with reserved budget → reconcile + persist effects (incl. partial/late) → `Scheduler.verify` (reuse current receipts) → required review once when proposed done (human path P3.5.2 / cell P4.4.3) → `commit_outcome_if_current` (contract version, candidate, execution generation; one ledger owner) → extractor enqueue (P4.2) → telemetry → `dispatch_outcome`: `done` close · `partial` continue same increment with register + seeds, no budget reset · `replan` replace plan with authorized coverage preserved · `waiting` persist handle + independent work · `blocked` resolve by authority or honest block · `failed` recover within original budget (ladder P4.6; P2: escalate to `blocked`) → `finish`.
- Done: scripted multi-increment campaign on a fixture repo; empty frontier with unverified requirements ends as `blocked`/`budget_exhausted`, never `completed`.
- Log: 2026-09-24 — `Controller.run(campaign, model, authority, syntax, maxCells)`: S0 ⇒ `runS0`; S1 ⇒ plan cell (`Roles.plan`, `CampaignProposals` intake on `packets`, `PlanPacketValidator` completion) ⇒ `PlanIntake.admit` ⇒ new `Transition.Planned(graph)` (validates; a replan keeps dispatched/verified/cancelled increments unchanged); loop: refusal check → ledger done ⇒ `stopOrFinish` → ready increment (none ⇒ regression refresh from current receipts, else honest stop) → cell cap ⇒ `budget_exhausted` → `CompileInputs(carry-forward of the increment's previous cell, hash-checked seeds)` → dispatch → shared `runCell` (extracted from `runS0`; carries the compiled `[K]` sections via the new `CellContext.sections`, the carried register and seeds, the role mask and proposal intake) → `Returned` → verifier on current receipts → `Committed` / continue / honest stop; finish receipt over every cell packet. `open` now runs S0 and S1 (S2/S3 ⇒ honest block) and stores a new contract with its selected shape; the Java/Kotlin facade calls `run`. D-70. Tests: `CampaignLoopTest` (plan + two increments ⇒ completed on receipts with I1's stale evidence refreshed from I2's AC-1 receipt; `maxCells = 1` ⇒ `budget_exhausted` with I2 pending), `ControllerTest` (S1 opens running; S2 with a `review:` item and no reviewer ⇒ blocked, again on reopen). Not yet: role switch rebuild (P2.2.3), full resume (P2.2.4), KB notes/contracts index in S1 compiles (`EmptyKb` until a host store), plan-packet decisions from the plan cell's register.

#### P2.2.4 [M] `Resume` protocol · DONE
- Why: [§13.4](docs/operations/recovery.md#sec-13-4); crash intervals; F12.
- Deps: P2.5.1, P1.4.3, P1.6.5 (handles)
- Build: `Resume.run(store, workspace)`: load contract, ledger, last register, Workset export, live handles, frozen attempt configuration; diff current stamp vs last recorded ⇒ `Touched (external)`; handles ⇒ `running | exited | lost` (identity key, not PID alone); open intents without receipts ⇒ `unknown_outcome` ⇒ reconcile before any retry; recompute receipt applicability by closure; delegated results with moved bases ⇒ `stale-for-integration` (P4/P5); `Rebuild(Resume)`; first turn sees `KNOWN: seeds only` + one-line resume note; register never trusted over the workspace; granted authorization preserved.
- Done: FX-22 (poll the same handle, no duplicate launch), FX-23 (both crash points), the four §13.4 crash intervals via `FaultInjector`.
- Log: 2026-09-24 — Resume = `Controller.open` reconciliation (P1.9.2: contract/ledger/state loaded, the frozen attempt config, stamp drift ⇒ `Touched (external)`, uncommitted intents ⇒ `unknown_outcome`, a running cell ⇒ `lost`) plus, new: every persisted background handle is reattached by identity and journaled `running|exited|deadline_exceeded|cancelled|lost` (`Reconciliation.handles`); a cell continuing an increment whose last cell was lost or interrupted — S0 and S1 — starts from that cell's validated carry-forward (register never trusted over the tree; seeds only) and a pinned one-line `resumed: …` note naming the stamp, external moves, unknown outcomes (reconcile before retry), handles (poll, never relaunch) and `KNOWN: seeds only`; `runCell` takes pinned messages. Granted authorization is the stored contract's, unchanged. `ResumeTest`: death during a background command ⇒ reopen lists `handle-1`, the resumed cell polls it, one `handles` row (FX-22); a dispatched intent whose acknowledgement was lost ⇒ `unknown_outcome`, named in the note, never replayed (FX-23 second point); death right after an in-cell pressure rebuild ⇒ journaled rebuild, turn-1 checkpoint settled, carry-forward seeds-only. With `VerticalSliceTest` (death between edit and receipt, FX-23 first point) the four §13.4 intervals are covered. Delegated results with moved bases stay P4/P5.

#### P2.2.6 [M] Campaign finish · DONE
- Why: [§8.7 campaign gate](docs/verification/acceptance-review.md#sec-8-7), [§7.3 cadence](docs/repository/navigation.md#sec-7-3), [§5.9 finish receipt](docs/runtime/gates-termination.md#sec-5-9).
- Build: `Controller.finish()`: all contract acceptance at the final stamp; full suite green or failures in the pre-existing ledger (allowed exceptions explicit); full-suite cadence every `K = 5` verified increments and at campaign end; campaign review predicate `(shape ≥ S2 ∧ increments ≥ 3) ∨ refactor_mode ∨ explicitly required` (human path P3 / cell P4); full `FinishReceipt`; explicit gaps otherwise.

Cadence: the full suite also runs every `FULL_SUITE_EVERY = 5` verified increments while work remains, a red result journaled. The S0 finish keeps D-66 (no full suite). The finish receipt covers every cell packet (P2.2.2). `CampaignLoopTest`: the two-increment campaign's finish runs the suite (green from a counted pytest log on POSIX, none declared on Windows); an increment that verifies before its last edit never completes the campaign; the review predicate's three branches. Comparing a red suite against the pre-existing-failure ledger waits for the S1 baseline (P3.1).

#### P2.3.3 [M] Hard admission check before every dispatch · DONE
- Why: [§6.1](docs/context/compiler.md#sec-6-1) total-context admission incl. output/reasoning headroom; re-estimated on actual usage; [§15.1](docs/platform/adapters.md#sec-15-1) effective continuation history counts.
- Build: `Admission.check(request, profile, estimate: Estimate, lastActualUsage) → ok | CapacityCondition`: planning estimates (D-06) are not the admission bound — admission uses the profile's tokenizer identity where available, otherwise the estimate plus its declared margin, and records exact-vs-estimated per request; effective/opaque continuation history counts or, if its size is unknown, forces a fresh lineage or a capacity result (never a silent fit); provider rejection is recorded as an estimation miss and the margin adjusted; multi-result turns re-estimated; capacity ⇒ pressure rebuild (P2.5.2) or explicit capacity condition, never a malformed history (I-17).
- Done: fake adapter with a small context limit triggers rebuild, never an oversize request; adversarial text and unknown continuation history are never marked exact-fit (IX-17).
- Log: 2026-09-24 — `context/ContextAdmission.kt`: `check(request, estimate)` → `Admitted | Capacity(OverWindow | UnknownHistory)` with an `AdmissionRecord` per request (estimate, bound, output headroom, exact?, learned margin): bound = the exact count only when the estimate is exact, else upper bound + learned margin; bound + `maxOutputTokens` must fit the profile window; replayed history of unknown size is never a fit (fresh lineage or capacity). `rejected(estimate)` (provider size rejection) and `observed(estimate, actualInput)` (under-estimate) widen the learned margin. Wired into `Cell` before the budget admission of every dispatch (one per lineage via `CellContext.admission`); capacity ⇒ `partial(Pressure)` with a replan hint until `Rebuild(Pressure)` (P2.5.2); adapter `ContextOverflow` rejections feed `rejected`, complete usage feeds `observed`. `ContextAdmissionTest`: IX-17 (adversarial text inexact, unknown continuation ⇒ capacity, misses widen the margin), exact fit to the token with output headroom, tiny window ⇒ pressure stop with zero dispatched requests.
